# Oteryn Game / Canary parity inventory — appendix: Crystal-only surface

Appendix of [the parity inventory](OTERYN_GAME_CANARY_PARITY_INVENTORY_2026-10-25.md). Status semantics and limits are defined there.

## 6. Crystal-only surface

Exact-key and paired-pack relative-path set differences are mechanically complete for the enumerated XML, enum, opcode, message and script populations. An additional path or pack variant can be a copied/moved script or a data-pack alternative; that alone does not prove Canary lacks the underlying mechanic. Such cases are explicitly PARTIAL candidates. This section does not assert a whole-server absence proof outside the requested source surfaces. Suggested ids are proposals, not allocated tasks or owner decisions.

No additional Crystal in-memory ItemAttribute enum names or read-attribute names were found. Canary-only read/enum differences remain in section 2.

No effective Crystal-only packet values remain after including both module registries. Crystal C++ `0x5F` maps to `parseBountyTaskAction`; Canary already owns `0x5F` via Task Board Lua. This is a source-owner/behaviour difference, recorded in section 3, rather than an absent Canary mechanic.
